Theorem 213.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the conservation object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 401. The moduli space has dimension at most 280 with 4 connected components.

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 401 + O(1/n^280). Summing over all modes and applying the conservation convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.

This completes the proof. QED.
